This Directory is published and distributed as a service to the Section of Patent, Trademark and Copyright Law of the American Bar Association by The Bureau of National Affairs, Inc. (BNA) 

The Bureau of National Affairs, Inc. (BNA) is the publisher of The United 

States Patents Quarterly, a unique information service for active legal practitioners concerned with the protection of creative assets. Since 1929, USPQ has been a timely and reliable source of decisional information which is invaluable in obtaining a patent, protecting a copyright or trademark, appealing a Patent Office decision, formulating a royalty agreement or license, or enjoining unfair competition. USPQ's service includes "Weekly Advance Sheets" which provide prompt reporting of the latest decisions, generally long before information regarding them is available from other sources. 

BNA makes available the following information services and reports:
